### [101] [Universal dynamics from a single-particle dark state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16494)
*Ruben Daraban,Arghavan Safavi-Naini,Johannes Schachenmayer,Mohammad Maghrebi*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维自旋链在相邻位点关联耗散下的多体动力学，发现单粒子零动量暗态在多体层面通过耗散诱导非线性被“穿衣服”，导致其缓慢衰减（1/log t），动量分布呈现k√t标度形式，总密度按1/(√t log t)衰减，并通过矩阵乘积态模拟验证了普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放量子系统中的暗态（亚辐射态）在少激发区域已被广泛研究，但其对多体动力学的影响尚不明确。本文旨在填补这一空白，探究单粒子暗态如何影响多体系统的长时行为。

Method: 理论分析结合矩阵乘积态（MPS）数值模拟。具体包括：（1）构建相邻位点关联耗散的自旋链模型；（2）分析单粒子零动量暗态在多体框架下的衰变机制；（3）推导动量分布和总密度的标度律；（4）引入软核相互作用验证普适性。

Result: （1）单粒子暗态被多体效应“穿衣服”后以1/log t缓慢衰减；（2）动量分布呈现k√t的普适标度形式；（3）总密度按1/(√t log t)衰减；（4）软核相互作用下相同行为依然成立，验证了普适性；（5）解释了近期工作中相互矛盾的结论的起源。

Conclusion: 单粒子暗态在多体系统中不再严格“暗”，而是表现出普适的慢衰减动力学，其标度行为对相互作用形式不敏感，揭示了耗散诱导非线性在多体开放系统中的关键作用。

Abstract: Open quantum systems can host dark or subradiant states whose decay is highly suppressed. While these states have been extensively studied in the few-excitation regime, their impact on the many-body dynamics remains largely unexplored. Here, we study a spin chain subject to correlated dissipation on neighboring sites, which admits a single-particle dark state at zero momentum. We show that the single-particle dark state qualitatively alters the many-body dynamics at long times, and identify its distinct universal behavior. While the zero-momentum mode is dark at the single-particle level, it decays slowly as $1/\log t$ as it becomes dressed by other modes through a dissipation-induced nonlinearity. We demonstrate that the momentum distribution takes a universal scaling form in $k\sqrt{t}$, and the total density decays as $1/\sqrt{t}\log t$. Our results further elucidate the origin of the conflicting results in recent works. Finally, we corroborate the analytics with matrix product state simulations and show that the same universal behavior persists for soft-core interactions, underscoring the universality of the emergent dynamics.

</details>


### [102] [Temperature-Controlled Resonance in a Heteronuclear Quantum Gas Mixture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17931)
*Xiaoyi Yang,Tianyu Xu,Shengli Ma,Zhigang Wu,Ren Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文提出通过控制异核混合气体的温度来实现连续可调的单通道共振（TCR）机制，揭示了费米面热展宽对杂质间有效势的调控作用，并解释了近期实验中观测到的温度相关损耗特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单通道共振在原子散射中是基本过程，但其发生通常是偶然的，缺乏系统性的调控手段。

Method: 将类Casimir介质相互作用推广到有限温度，利用费米面的热展宽重塑杂质间的有效势，从而产生温度控制的共振（TCR）。

Result: 共振位置随温度变化系统性地移动，提供了清晰的实验信号；TCR机制与近期实验中观测到的温度依赖性损耗特征一致。

Conclusion: 温度可作为超冷量子气体中单通道共振的简单且实验上易操作的控制旋钮。

Abstract: Single-channel resonances are fundamental processes in scattering of atoms, yet their occurrence is largely incidental and lacks systematic control. In this Letter, we propose a mechanism to realize a continuously tunable single-channel resonance by controlling the temperature of the heteronuclear mixture. By extending the Casimir-like mediated interaction to finite temperature, we demonstrate that thermal smearing of the Fermi surface reshapes the effective potential between impurities, giving rise to a temperature-controlled resonance (TCR) over a wide parameter range. As a direct consequence, the resonance position shifts systematically with temperature variation, providing a clear experimental signature of this mechanism. We further investigate the quench dynamics of a Bose gas immersed in a Fermi sea and demonstrate that the observed temperature-dependent loss features in recent experiments are consistent with the TCR mechanism. Our results establish temperature as a simple and experimentally accessible control knob for single-channel resonances in ultracold quantum gases.

</details>


### [103] [Shapiro steps of superfluid Fermi gases in a ring trap across the BCS--BEC crossover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17940)
*Hikaru Kuriki,Masaya Kunimi,Tetsuro Nikuni*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文通过求解含时Bogoliubov-de Gennes方程，研究了环状势阱中超流费米气体在移动势垒下的输运性质，发现在BCS到BEC交叉区域存在清晰的Shapiro台阶，其化学势差量子化单位为ħω/2，并揭示这些台阶源于势垒处周期性孤子产生所介导的同步相位滑移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究环状阱中超流费米气体从BCS到BEC整个交叉区域中，移动势垒所诱导的非平衡相动力学特性，特别是Shapiro台阶的存在性及其微观机制。

Method: 采用含时Bogoliubov-de Gennes (BdG)方程，模拟受直流和交流电流偏置的约瑟夫森结动力学过程，覆盖BCS到BEC交叉区域的宽相互作用强度范围。

Result: 在相位相干区域内观察到低阶Shapiro台阶，其时均化学势差以ħω/2为单位量子化；微观分析表明这些台阶由势垒处周期性孤子产生介导的同步相位滑移导致。

Conclusion: 阐明了不同相互作用区域在环状阱费米子超流体的非平衡相动力学中的作用，为未来具有非平庸拓扑的原子电子学系统研究提供了微观层面的见解。

Abstract: We investigate the transport properties of a superfluid Fermi gas confined in a ring trap with a moving potential barrier across the Bardeen-Cooper-Schrieffer (BCS) to Bose-Einstein condensate (BEC) crossover. Employing time-dependent Bogoliubov--de Gennes (BdG) equations, we simulate the dynamics of a Josephson junction biased by both DC and AC currents. Over a wide range of interaction strengths, we observe clear low-order Shapiro-step plateaus in the barrier-velocity--chemical-potential-diffenrence, within the phase-coherent regime, where the time-averaged chemical potential difference is quantized in units of $\hbarω/2$. This factor of $1/2$ reflects our convention of defining the chemical potential per single fermion in the BdG framework. Microscopic analysis reveals that these fundamental steps originate from synchronized phase slips mediated by periodic soliton generation at the barrier. Our findings clarify the role of interaction regimes in the nonequilibrium phase dynamics of ring-trapped fermionic superfluids and provide microscopic insights relevant to future studies of atomtronic systems with nontrivial topology.

</details>


### [104] [Weak Fragmentation and Thermalization in a Dipole-Conserving Bose-Hubbard Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18119)
*Chenrong Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维偶极守恒玻色-哈伯德链中的希尔伯特空间碎裂与热化问题，发现系统存在弱希尔伯特空间碎裂，并构建了指数级数量的冻结乘积态。通过精确对角化分析，揭示了从弱本征态热化到非遍历态随相互作用增强的转变，表明弱碎裂不会阻止量子混沌或热化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究偶极守恒系统中希尔伯特空间碎裂的性质及其对热化的影响，特别是弱碎裂是否阻碍量子混沌和热化。

Method: 通过分析Fock基下哈密顿量矩阵结构识别弱碎裂；利用组合构造方法推导冻结乘积态数量的上下界；采用精确对角化计算本征态纠缠熵、密度弛豫动力学和能级统计量。

Result: 系统展现弱希尔伯特空间碎裂；存在指数级数量的冻结乘积态；随着在位相互作用增强，系统从弱本征态热化区域过渡到非遍历区域。

Conclusion: 弱希尔伯特空间碎裂不会阻止量子混沌或热化，为研究偶极守恒、弱碎裂与遍历性破缺之间的相互作用提供了最小化平台。

Abstract: We study Hilbert-space fragmentation and thermalization in a one-dimensional dipole-conserving Bose-Hubbard chain. By analyzing the structure of the Hamiltonian matrix in the Fock basis, we show that the system exhibits weak Hilbert-space fragmentation. We further construct an exponentially large family of frozen product states and derive analytical upper and lower bounds on their number. Using exact diagonalization, we examine the consequences of weak fragmentation for eigenstate half-chain entanglement, density relaxation dynamics, and level statistics. All these quantities reveal a transition from a weak eigenstate thermalization regime to a nonergodic regime with increasing on-site interaction strength. These results show that weak Hilbert-space fragmentation \textit{does not} preclude quantum chaos or thermalization, and provides a minimal platform for studying the interplay of dipole conservation, weak fragmentation, and ergodicity breaking.

</details>


### [105] [Confinement-controlled pattern selection in a finite population-imbalanced dipolar Bose-Einstein condensate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18139)
*Zhenhao Wang,Weijing Bao,Jia-Rui Luo,Gentaro Watanabe,Kui-Tian Xi*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究了圆盒限制下人口不平衡的双组分偶极玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体的基态密度图案，发现其支持从均匀煎饼态到液滴阵列、同心环等多种驻态形态，这些图案与嵌段共聚物中的微相分离形态高度对应，且特征图案间距与轴向约束长度呈线性关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解受限几何下人口不平衡偶极玻色混合物的基态相图，探索其与经典嵌段共聚物体系在微相分离形态上的结构对应关系。

Method: 采用平均场模型，系统扫描轴向约束强度、相互作用不平衡度和人口比例三个参数空间，绘制相图，并通过密度轮廓和结构因子分析特征波矢与图案间距的标度行为。

Result: 发现了均匀煎饼态、煎饼-液滴共存态、环-液滴共存态、液滴阵列和同心环等多种形态；人口不平衡扮演了类似嵌段共聚物体积分数的角色；调制态具有固有的非零特征波矢；图案间距与轴向约束长度呈线性关系，但在有限圆盒中该缩放被整数量子化的台阶所中断。

Conclusion: 盒捕获的偶极混合物为研究量子流体中的有限尺寸图案选择和非局域微相形成提供了可控实验平台。

Abstract: We study the ground-state density patterns of a population-imbalanced two-component dipolar Bose-Einstein condensate confined in a circular quasi-two-dimensional box. Using a mean-field model, we map out phase diagrams as functions of the axial confinement, interaction imbalance, and population ratio. The system supports a rich sequence of stationary morphologies, including a nearly uniform pancake state, pancake-droplet and ring-droplet coexistence states, droplet arrays, and concentric rings. These patterns show a close structural correspondence to microphase-separated morphologies in diblock-copolymer systems, with the population imbalance acting as an effective volume fraction that selects the pattern topology. Analysis of the density profiles and structure factors reveals that the modulated states possess an intrinsic nonzero characteristic wave vector, which remains essentially unchanged when the box size is varied. We also find that the characteristic pattern spacing scales linearly with the axial confinement length, indicating that the transverse thickness of the condensate controls the effective in-plane length scale. In a finite circular box, this smooth scaling is interrupted by discrete steps, reflecting geometric frustration and the integer locking of the number of rings or droplets. Our results show that box-trapped dipolar mixtures provide a controllable platform for studying finite-size pattern selection and nonlocal microphase formation in quantum fluids.

</details>


### [106] [Jones-Roberts solitary waves and the onset of rotation in a spherical surface condensate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18297)
*Noel Cuadra,Thomas Gasenzer,Davide Proment,Alberto Villois*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究了稀释玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体在薄球壳上旋转起始的非线性激发，发现这些激发对应绕球体匀速旋转的孤立波：低速时为反向涡旋偶极子，高速时演变为无涡的Jones-Roberts孤子，进一步加速则与赤道局域模式混合，其传播速度作为Landau临界速度限制了JR孤子族的最大角速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索稀释玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体在薄球壳几何中旋转起始的非线性激发机制，理解孤立波从涡旋偶极子到Jones-Roberts孤子的演化过程及其速度上限的物理起源。

Method: 通过理论分析研究球壳上匀速旋转的孤立波解，涵盖低速涡旋偶极子、高速Jones-Roberts孤子以及更高速度下与赤道局域模式的杂交行为，并将模式传播速度与Landau临界速度建立关联。

Result: 发现了从涡旋偶极子到Jones-Roberts孤子的连续演化路径；确定了赤道局域模式的波数由球壳半径与愈合长度的比值决定；证明了Jones-Roberts孤子族存在由Landau临界速度设定的角速度上限。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了球壳几何中BEC旋转激发的完整图景，建立了非线性激发与Landau临界速度之间的定量联系，为理解有限温度下球面超流体的动力学行为提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The nonlinear excitations underlying the onset of rotation in a dilute Bose-Einstein condensate confined to a thin spherical shell are studied. These excitations correspond to solitary waves rotating about the sphere at constant angular speed: at low speeds they appear as dipoles of singly quantized vortices with opposite circulation, while at higher speeds they evolve into vortex-free Jones-Roberts solitons. With further increase of the angular speed, these excitations hybridize with equatorially confined modes whose azimuthal wave number is set by the sphere radius measured in units of the healing length. The propagation speed of these modes is shown to play the role of a Landau critical velocity, thereby setting the upper limiting angular speed of the entire Jones-Roberts family.

</details>


### [107] [A study of the dimer-trimer crossover in a driven three-component Fermi gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18405)
*Ronnie Slowinski,Gaël Servignat,Frédéric Chevy,Carlos Lobo*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文构建了一种包含三种可区分原子物种的低能有效场论（EFT），研究了真空中和单个费米海背景下二体和三体的结合能。通过外部驱动调控前两种原子间的相互作用产生闭通道二聚体，并将其与第三种原子间的接触相互作用进行建模。推导了真空中和介质中二聚体与三聚体结合能的解析表达式，预测了介质中二聚体和三聚体分支在原子-二聚体散射长度变化时的交叉行为，并表明该交叉位置可通过外部驱动调节的原子-原子耦合控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立一种可独立调控不同原子间相互作用的有效场论框架，研究多体环境中二聚体和三聚体束缚态的演化行为，特别是介质诱导的束缚态分支交叉现象及其调控机制。

Method: 采用有效场论（EFT）方法，将外部驱动下前两种原子间的相互作用建模为可独立调控的闭通道二聚体，其余相互作用（二聚体与第三种原子）以接触相互作用描述。通过变分法计算真空中和存在单个费米海背景下的二体和三体结合能，并推导解析表达式。

Result: 推导了真空中和介质中二聚体与三聚体结合能的解析表达式；预测了介质中二聚体与三聚体分支在原子-二聚体散射长度变化时存在交叉行为，该交叉位置可通过外部驱动调节的原子-原子耦合进行控制。

Conclusion: 建立了包含三种可区分原子物种的可调控有效场论框架，揭示了介质中二聚体与三聚体分支的交叉现象及其调控机制，该交叉行为类似于通常的极化子-分子问题，为多体束缚态调控提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We develop an Effective Field Theory (EFT) for a system with three distinguishable atomic species and present a variational calculation of the two and three-body binding energies in vacuum and in the presence of a single Fermi sea. Specifically, we consider the case where the interaction between first two atomic species is externally driven so as to produce a (non-universal) closed-channel dimer whose coupling can be controlled independently of all other interactions. We then model the remaining interactions as a contact interaction between the dimer and a third atomic species which forms the medium. We derive analytical expressions for the dimer and trimer binding energies in vacuum and in medium, and in the latter case we predict a crossing between the dimer and trimer branches as a function of the atom-dimer scattering length, analogous to the usual polaron and molecule problem. Furthermore, we show that the position of this crossing can be controlled by varying the atom-atom coupling that results from the external drive and we discuss the implications of these findings.

</details>


### [108] [Bilayer crystals in a polar-molecules system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18546)
*Vinicius Zampronio,Matteo Ciardi,Fabio Cinti*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文通过量子蒙特卡洛模拟，研究了准二维谐波束缚下极性分子的有限温度相图，发现了正常流体、超流、超固态、团簇晶体和双层晶体等多种量子相，揭示了热涨落在诱导结晶中的非平凡作用，并展示了通过调控束缚强度稳定双层晶体及层间超流相干性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索极性分子在准二维几何中由谐波势束缚下的有限温度相图，揭示热涨落与相互作用、束缚强度之间的耦合机制，特别是在强关联区域中可实验实现的新奇量子相。

Method: 采用量子蒙特卡洛（Quantum Monte Carlo）模拟方法，研究沿极化轴方向受谐波势束缚的准二维极性分子系统，通过调节温度和束缚强度扫描相图。

Result: 识别出正常流体、超流、超固态、团簇晶体、双层晶体等丰富相态；发现升温可诱导结晶；通过改变束缚强度可稳定每格点单分子的双层晶体；观测到具有层间相位相干的层状超流态。

Conclusion: 这些发现揭示了低维偶极系统中相互作用、束缚与温度之间的相互影响，为利用超冷极性分子工程化量子相提供了新方向。

Abstract: We investigate the finite-temperature phase diagram of polar molecules confined in a quasi-two-dimensional geometry by a harmonic potential along the polarization axis. We employ Quantum Monte Carlo simulations to explore the strongly correlated regime accessible with current experimental setups. By tuning temperature and confinement strength, we identify a rich set of phases, including normal fluid, superfluid, supersolid, cluster crystal, and bilayer crystal states. Our results reveal the emergence of crystallization upon increasing temperature, highlighting the nontrivial role of thermal fluctuations in dipolar systems. In particular, we show that a bilayer crystal with one molecule per lattice site can be stabilized by varying the confinement strength at fixed interaction. Moreover, we show evidence of layering of superfluid states with phase coherence between the two layers. These findings provide insight into the interplay between interactions, confinement, and temperature in low-dimensional dipolar systems, and suggest new directions for engineering quantum phases with ultracold polar molecules.

</details>


### [109] [Can machine learning for quantum-gas experiments be explainable?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18689)
*I. B. Spielman amd J. P. Zwolak*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文展示了机器学习在冷原子量子模拟器图像处理中的两个应用：原始图像去噪和玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体中孤子波的识别，并讨论了性能、模型复杂度和可解释性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体原子物理学的实验技术难度高、数据集庞大，且经典模拟量子系统的计算资源需求随系统规模指数增长，亟需更高效的数据处理方法。

Method: 采用机器学习方法对冷原子量子模拟器产生的图像数据进行处理，具体包括：1）原始图像去噪；2）玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体中孤子波的识别。

Result: ML方法在图像去噪和孤子波识别两个任务上均取得了有效的性能表现。

Conclusion: 机器学习有望变革多体原子物理领域，但需在模型性能、复杂度与可解释性之间进行合理权衡。

Abstract: Virtually all aspects of many-body atomic physics are challenging: experiments are technically demanding, datasets have become enormous, and the memory and CPU requirements for classical simulation of generic quantum systems often scale exponentially with system size. Machine learning (ML) methods are already assisting in each of these areas and are poised to become transformative. Here, we focus on two specific applications of ML to cold-atom-based quantum simulators. These devices generally generate data in the form of images; we first showcase denoising of raw images and then identify solitonic waves in Bose-Einstein condensates. In both of these examples, we comment on the interplay between performance, model complexity, and interpretability.

</details>


### [110] [Resonant two-cluster scattering in a quasi-one-dimensional Bose gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28250)
*Tomohiro Tanaka,Yusuke Nishida*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究准一维玻色气体中的两团簇散射，重点分析由横向约束诱导的有效三体相互作用——这是破坏准一维体系中可积性的主导项。利用Lüscher公式和Lieb-Liniger玻色气体的可积性，发现弹性两团簇散射的散射长度有限且为正，表明存在共振现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究准一维玻色气体中由横向约束诱导的有效三体相互作用对两团簇散射的影响，以及该相互作用如何破坏体系的可积性并导致共振。

Method: 采用Lüscher公式与Lieb-Liniger玻色气体的可积性理论相结合的方法，计算弹性两团簇散射的散射长度。

Result: 弹性两团簇散射的散射长度为有限正值，且该散射长度表明存在共振现象。

Conclusion: 准一维玻色气体中，由横向约束诱导的有效三体相互作用可导致两团簇散射出现共振，这为理解准一维可积性破缺机制提供了重要依据。

Abstract: We investigate two-cluster scattering in a quasi-one-dimensional Bose gas. We focus on the effective three-body interaction induced by transverse confinement, which is the leading term for breaking integrability in the quasi-one-dimensional setting. Exploiting the Lüscher formula and the integrability of the Lieb-Liniger Bose gas, we find a finite and positive scattering length for elastic two-cluster scattering. The resulting scattering lengths indicate the emergence of a resonance.

</details>

### [111] [Correlation-Driven Spin Reorientation via Competing Anisotropy Channels in CrPS4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16541)
*Raju Baral,Harald O. Jeschke,Igor I. Mazin,Jue Liu,David Mandrus,Stuart Calder*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过磁对分布函数（mPDF）分析揭示了准一维范德瓦尔斯反铁磁体CrPS₄中温度诱导自旋重取向的关联驱动机制，发现铁磁链内关联在Néel温度以上持续存在，结合DFT自旋哈密顿量阐明了单离子各向异性和交换各向异性通道的差异化重整化导致有效易轴旋转，并指出了主导链近似的极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解准一维范德瓦尔斯反铁磁体CrPS₄中温度诱导自旋重取向的微观机制，特别是低维磁关联如何控制磁各向异性。

Method: 采用磁对分布函数（mPDF）分析解析局域自旋方向，结合DFT导出的自旋哈密顿量，研究单离子各向异性与交换各向异性通道的竞争及关联退化的影响。

Result: 发现铁磁链内关联在TN以上持续存在；单离子各向异性保持局域性，而交换各向异性随关联退化被重整化，导致有效易轴旋转并捕捉到有序态倾角；在TN以上自旋继续旋转，揭示了主导链近似的局限性。

Conclusion: mPDF导出的关联可作为微观哈密顿量的直接输入，低维关联能有效控制磁各向异性，为理解低维磁系统中的自旋重取向机制提供了新范式。

Abstract: We identify a correlation-driven mechanism for the temperature-induced spin reorientation in the quasi-one-dimensional van der Waals antiferromagnet CrPS4. Magnetic pair distribution function (mPDF) analysis resolves the local spin direction and shows that ferromagnetic intrachain correlations persist far above TN. Combining these correlations with a DFT-derived spin Hamiltonian reveals competing single-ion and exchange-anisotropy channels, with single-ion anisotropy remaining local while exchange anisotropy is renormalized as intersite correlations decay. This differential renormalization rotates the effective easy axis and captures the ordered-state canting. Above TN, the continued rotation beyond the model prediction delineates the limits of the dominant-chain approximation. These results establish mPDF-derived correlations as direct inputs to microscopic Hamiltonians and show how low-dimensional correlations can control magnetic anisotropy.

</details>


### [112] [Revealing Hund superdispersion with tunneling spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16580)
*Luke C. Rhodes,Fabian B. Kugler,Olivier Gingras,Carolina Marques,Edgar Abarca Morales,Phil D. C. King,Antoine Georges,Peter Wahl*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过隧道光谱实验在Sr₂RuO₄中发现了Hund耦合导致的“超色散”谱特征，结合DFT、DMFT和连续局域态密度计算，证实这些特征与Hund金属中自能实部的非单调能量依赖密切相关，为多轨道系统中的Hund物理提供了直接实验证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铜氧化物超导体中电子-电子排斥导致“瀑布”光谱特征，但在多轨道系统中，Hund耦合会产生超越传统Mott-Hubbard范式的“超色散”特征，需要实验验证这一理论预测。

Method: 使用隧道光谱技术测量Sr₂RuO₄，结合密度泛函理论（DFT）、动力学平均场理论（DMFT）和连续局域态密度计算进行理论分析。

Result: 实验观测到的光谱特征与理论预测高度一致，证明这些特征与Hund金属中自能实部的非单调能量依赖密切相关。

Conclusion: 提供了Hund诱导光谱特征的直接实验证据，为探测量子材料中的关联效应开辟了新途径。

Abstract: In cuprate superconductors, electron-electron repulsion results in characteristic spectroscopic features known as `waterfalls', where the sharp quasiparticle dispersion transitions into broad Hubbard bands. However, in multi-orbital systems, the additional Hund coupling results in behavior that defies the conventional Mott--Hubbard paradigm, creating qualitatively distinct `superdispersive' features in the spectral function. Here, we use tunneling spectroscopy to reveal this signature of Hund physics in Sr$_2$RuO$_4$. By combining density functional theory, dynamical mean-field theory, and continuum local density of states calculations, we show that the experimental features are in excellent agreement with theoretical predictions and intimately linked to the non-monotonous energy dependence of the real part of the self-energy in a Hund metal. Our results provide direct experimental evidence for Hund-induced spectroscopic features and open a new route to probing correlation effects in quantum materials.

</details>


### [113] [Finite-frequency anomaly-induced electromechanical response of Dirac fermions in deformed graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17632)
*Ara Sedrakyan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了石墨烯在低能Dirac理论中形变产生的几何电子-声子顶点，特别是作为规范场的声子场与电磁矢势耦合导致的机电响应，发现移动形变可产生横向电流，形变模式可束缚电荷，并给出了清晰的实验可观测信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索石墨烯中形变如何通过几何电子-声子顶点（特别是声子规范场）产生直接的机电耦合效应，揭示从机械形变到电子输运的物理机制。

Method: 基于低能Dirac理论分析声子规范场的耦合，计算混合机电响应的系数（奇异的流-流关联函数），区分绝缘锥（Chern-Simons值）和掺杂锥（Berry曲率因子修正）两种情形，并应用于具体形变模式（行波弯曲波、静态波纹+动态声子、非共线模式）。

Result: 行波弯曲波产生横向二次谐波电流；静态波纹混合动态声子产生驱动频率下的横向电流；两个非共线模式通过声子通量产生电荷调制。对于子晶格有能隙的石墨烯，两个谷相加而非抵消，实验特征为横向电信号在二倍弯曲波频率处出现，相位由子晶格能隙符号决定，栅压依赖从能隙平台到1/|μ|衰减。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了方向、相位、频率和栅压的选择定则，可作为干净地检验形变石墨烯中反常诱导的机电通道的实验探针。

Abstract: A deformation of a graphene sheet changes more than the positions of the atoms. In the low-energy Dirac theory it also produces geometric electron-phonon vertices. One of these vertices acts as an emergent phonon gauge field, $\calA_μ$, which couples to the same Dirac current as the electromagnetic vector potential. This shared current vertex gives a direct route from mechanics to electronics: a moving deformation can generate a transverse electric current, and a deformation pattern with emergent phonon flux can bind electric charge. We show that the coefficient of this mixed electromechanical response is the parity-odd current-current correlator of a massive Dirac cone. For an insulating cone the coefficient is the one-cone Chern-Simons value, while for a doped cone in the local regime it is reduced by the Berry curvature factor $m/|μ|$. We apply the response to explicit deformations. A traveling flexural wave generates a transverse second-harmonic current; a static ripple mixed with a dynamic phonon generates a transverse current at the drive frequency; and two non-collinear modes can generate charge modulation through the emergent phonon flux. We keep the spin and valley sum explicit, so the paper shows when the one-cone anomaly becomes a charge current in graphene and when it instead appears in a valley, spin, or spin-valley channel. For sublattice-gapped graphene with a valley-odd deformation gauge coupling, the two valleys add rather than cancel. The experimentally sharp signature is a transverse electrical signal at twice the flexural-wave frequency, with a phase fixed by the sign of the sublattice gap and a gate dependence that crosses over from a gap plateau to a $1/|μ|$ decay. These direction, phase, frequency, and gate-voltage selection rules give clean tests of the anomaly-induced electromechanical channel in deformed graphene.

</details>


### [114] [Systematic dynamical mean-field theory study of 3d perovskite oxides with uniform Coulomb interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16771)
*Antik Sihi,Caden Ginter,Kristjan Haule,Subhasish Mandal*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高通量eDMFT框架，通过使用物理推导的固定库仑相互作用参数（金属U=6 eV，绝缘体U=10 eV）和精确双计数，无需针对每种材料单独调参，即可准确计算强关联过渡金属氧化物ABO₃的电子结构，计算结果与光电子实验高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DFT+DMFT方法虽能准确描述强关联过渡金属钙钛矿氧化物，但其高通量应用受限于需要确定材料特定的库仑相互作用参数U。第一性原理方法（如cRPA）预测的U值在不同化学相似的ABO₃钙钛矿中呈现高度非线性和不可迁移性，制约了基于数据驱动的强关联材料发现。

Method: 采用大能量窗口的eDMFT方法，使用高度局域化轨道并在同一多体框架内自洽处理电子关联和屏蔽效应。基于约束eDMFT计算发现自洽屏蔽的库仑相互作用在关联金属和绝缘体中自然落在较窄范围内，因此直接采用固定U值（金属6 eV，绝缘体10 eV）结合精确双计数，对ABO₃（A=Ca, Sr, La; B=V–Ni）进行系统高通量eDMFT计算。

Result: 计算得到的谱函数与光电子实验整体上高度一致，验证了该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 电荷自洽的eDMFT方法能够实现无需参数调优的高通量多体计算，为建立强关联材料的预测性电子结构数据库提供了可行的路径。

Abstract: Strongly correlated transition-metal perovskite oxides pose a fundamental challenge for electronic-structure theory and for large-scale, data-driven materials discovery. While DFT+DMFT provides a quantitatively accurate description of such systems, its high-throughput application is hindered by the need to determine material-specific Coulomb interaction parameters ($U$). First-principles approaches such as the cRPA predict a highly nonlinear and non-transferable evolution of the interaction strength across chemically similar ABO$_3$ perovskites. Here we show that this paradigm does not extend to the large-energy-window eDMFT, which employs highly localized orbitals and treats electronic correlations and screening self-consistently within the same many-body framework. As a result, spectral properties are governed primarily by the dynamical self-energy rather than by static interaction-induced energy shifts. Recent constrained-eDMFT calculations demonstrated that, for broad classes of $3d$ transition-metal oxides, the self-consistently screened Coulomb interactions naturally fall within relatively narrow ranges for correlated metals and insulators. Motivated by these findings, we implement a high-throughput eDMFT framework employing physically derived interaction values of $U=6$ eV for metals and $U=10$ eV for insulators together with $exact$ double counting. We test this framework using systematic high-throughput eDMFT calculations for ABO$_3$ compounds (A = Ca, Sr, La; B = V--Ni) and benchmark the resulting spectral functions against photoemission experiments, where we find overall excellent agreement. Our results establish that charge self-consistent eDMFT enables robust, parameter-tuning-free high-throughput many-body calculations for correlated oxides, opening a practical pathway toward predictive electronic-structure databases for strongly correlated materials.

</details>


### [115] [Finite-Temperature Spin Exchange-Correlation Kernel of the Uniform Electron Gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16888)
*Pengcheng Hou,Zhiyi Li,Youjin Deng,Kun Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用变分图解蒙特卡洛方法计算了均匀电子气在有限温度下的静态自旋交换关联核，涵盖从量子简并到经典区域，揭示了温度对自旋相关结构的抑制效应，并评估了热局域自旋密度近似的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀电子气的有限温度自旋响应是自旋极化/磁化电子液体（包括温稠密物质）的基础参考，但其约束远低于电荷响应，缺乏系统的高精度第一性原理数据。

Method: 采用变分图解蒙特卡洛（Variational Diagrammatic Monte Carlo）方法，计算无极化均匀电子气在金属密度下、涵盖量子简并、温稠密和经典三个区间的静态自旋交换关联核 K_{xc}(q;T)。

Result: 1) 自旋交换关联核在低温下与零温自旋响应参数化平滑连接；2) 加热抑制费米面尺度的自旋关联结构并削弱Stoner增强；3) 长波极限可检验热局域自旋密度近似（LSDA）的自旋刚度，低温一致但温稠密区域存在残余偏差；4) 经典区自旋XC核在费米动量尺度上近乎局域，与电荷XC核形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 该工作为有限温度自旋响应理论和磁化温稠密物质建模提供了第一性原理基础。

Abstract: The finite-temperature spin response of the uniform electron gas (UEG) is a fundamental reference for spin-polarized and magnetized electron liquids, including warm dense matter (WDM), yet it remains far less constrained than charge response. Using variational diagrammatic Monte Carlo, we compute the static spin exchange--correlation (XC) kernel $K_{xc}(q;T)$ of the unpolarized UEG at metallic densities across the quantum-degenerate, warm-dense, and classical regimes. The kernel connects smoothly to zero-temperature spin-response parametrizations at low temperature, while heating suppresses the Fermi-surface-scale spin-correlation structure and weakens the XC-driven Stoner enhancement. Its long-wavelength limit provides a direct response test of the spin stiffness implied by thermal local-spin-density-approximation (LSDA) parametrizations, showing low-temperature consistency while exposing a resolved warm-dense residual in current LSDA parametrizations. In the classical regime, the spin XC kernel becomes nearly local on the Fermi-momentum scale, in sharp contrast to the corresponding charge XC kernel. These results provide a first-principles basis for finite-temperature spin-response theory and magnetized WDM modeling.

</details>


### [116] [Frustration from Localized Zhang-Rice States: A Unified Theory of Doping-Driven Magnetic Transitions in Cuprates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18453)
*Xiaodong Wang,Ping Xu,Jiong Mei,Shao-Hang Shi,Zi-Xiang Li,Mingpu Qin,Kun Jiang,Hui-Ke Jin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出掺杂空穴形成局域化的Zhang-Rice单重态，这些态通过引入新的超交换路径（J₂和J₃）产生磁阻挫，从而解释了铜氧化物中反铁磁序的快速坍塌、自旋玻璃相的出现以及电子-空穴掺杂磁学不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解掺杂空穴破坏反铁磁序的微观机制是铜氧化物超导的核心问题之一，现有理论未能很好地解释空穴掺杂侧反铁磁序快速坍塌和自旋玻璃相的出现。

Method: 提出统一微观理论：掺杂空穴形成局域化的Zhang-Rice单重态，这些单重态并非简单的非磁性空位，而是主动引入次近邻J₂和第三近邻J₃超交换相互作用，从而产生磁阻挫。

Result: 该理论自然解释了空穴掺杂侧Néel反铁磁序的快速坍塌以及自旋玻璃相的出现，并为轻掺杂铜氧化物中复杂的掺杂驱动磁相变和电子-空穴磁学不对称性提供了统一框架。

Conclusion: 局域化Zhang-Rice单重态通过产生磁阻挫是理解铜氧化物掺杂磁学行为的关键机制，这一框架较以往将掺杂视为简单非磁性空位的理论更为全面。

Abstract: The microscopic mechanism by which doped holes disrupt the antiferromagnetic order is one of the fundamental questions in cuprates. In this work, we propose a unified microscopic theory in which doped holes form spatially localized Zhang-Rice singlets which actively mediate emergent spin exchange. Rather than acting as simple non-magnetic vacancies, these localized states introduce emergent next-nearest $J_2$ and third-nearest $J_3$ neighbor superexchanges. This dopant-induced exchange pathway generates significant magnetic frustration, naturally explaining the rapid collapse of the Néel AFM order and the emergence of a spin-glass phase on the hole-doped side. Our findings provide a comprehensive framework for understanding the complex doping-driven magnetic phase transitions and magnetic electron-hole asymmetry in lightly doped cuprates.

</details>


### [117] [Specific heat and susceptibility of S=1/2 antiferromagnets on square, triangular, and kagome lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17420)
*Chisa Hotta*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用正弦平方变形（SSD）方法，研究了二维自旋-1/2反铁磁体（三角、kagome、正方晶格及一维链）的热力学性质，发现kagome晶格在低温下比热呈双峰结构且磁化率显著增强，表明其低能激发主要由磁性态主导，而三角晶格则不然。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解受阻量子自旋系统中热力学性质的温度依赖性，特别是三角晶格和kagome晶格在低能激发特性上的差异。

Method: 对哈密顿量施加正弦平方变形（SSD），通过实空间包络函数使边界局域能量尺度平滑降至零，从而在小尺寸有限元计算中模拟热力学极限，计算能量密度、比热和磁化率随温度的变化。

Result: 三角晶格和kagome晶格反铁磁体在低温比热中均出现肩峰或明显双峰结构；kagome晶格在极低温下磁化率显著增强；而三角晶格的低能激发中磁性贡献较小。

Conclusion: 与正方晶格和一维链对比发现，尽管两个受阻系统均保留大量熵，但kagome晶格在~0.5J以下的低能激发主要由磁性激发态主导，三角晶格则不然。

Abstract: We study the temperature dependence of the thermodynamic properties of spin-1/2 antiferromagnets on two-dimensional lattices. Our analysis employs the sine-square deformation (SSD), in which a real-space envelope function is applied to the Hamiltonian so that the local energy scale is smoothly reduced to zero at the system boundaries. The quantum eigenstates of the SSD Hamiltonian exhibit bulk-like behavior near the system center, effectively mimicking the thermodynamic limit even in small finite-size calculations. Using these fictitious bulk states, we compute the energy density, specific heat, and magnetic susceptibility as functions of temperature. We find that both the triangular- and kagome-lattice antiferromagnets show either a shoulder or a pronounced double-peak structure in the low-temperature specific heat, whereas the kagome case particularly shows a strong enhancement of magnetic susceptibility down to the lowest temperature range. These direct comparisons, together with the square-lattice and one-dimensional cases, reveal that although both frustrated systems retain a substantial amount of entropy, the low-energy excitations below ~ 0.5J of the kagome lattice are predominantly governed by the magnetic excited states, whereas not much for the triangular lattice.

</details>


### [118] [Partial Kondo Screening Solves the Mystery of Rare Earth Tetraborides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17469)
*Soumyaranjan Dash,Sanjeev Kumar*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过混合和半经典蒙特卡洛模拟，在Shastry-Sutherland晶格的Kondo晶格模型中发现了多个磁化平台（1/6、2/9、1/4、1/3、1/2、2/3、3/4），并揭示了这些平台态来源于动能、Kondo耦合和磁阻挫三者之间的竞争，成功解释了稀土四硼化物中异常的磁输运现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决稀土四硼化物（RB₄）中长期以来关于变磁性和反常磁输运的难题，提出一种新的机制来解释观察到的多个磁化平台。

Method: 采用混合和半经典蒙特卡洛模拟方法，在Shastry-Sutherland晶格上对Kondo晶格模型进行研究。

Result: 发现1/6、2/9、1/4、1/3、1/2、2/3和3/4饱和磁化强度的稳健磁化平台；大多数平台态是部分Kondo屏蔽的，源于动能、Kondo耦合和磁阻挫的三方竞争；成功解释了ErB₄和TmB₄中异常磁输运行为。

Conclusion: 为RB₄中变磁性和反常磁输运提供了简洁优雅的解释，并引入了一种预测和发现阻挫Kondo晶格中新型关联相的新机制。

Abstract: We invoke a new mechanism to account for multiple magnetization plateaus observed in rare-earth tetraborides. Using a combination of hybrid and semiclassical Monte Carlo simulations of the Kondo lattice model (KLM) on the Shastry-Sutherland lattice (SSL), we find robust magnetization plateaus at fractions 1/6, 2/9, 1/4, 1/3, 1/2, 2/3 and 3/4 of the saturation magnetization. We find that most of the plateau states are partially Kondo screened and emerge from the field-tuning of a complex three-way competition between the kinetic energy, the Kondo coupling, and the magnetic frustration. Most remarkably, the unusual magneto-transport reported in ErB$_4$ and TmB$_4$ admits an unexpectedly simple explanation within our mechanism. This work not only provides an elegant and simple solution to the long-standing puzzle of metamagnetism and anomalous magnetotransport in RB$_4$, but also introduces a novel mechanism to predict and discover new correlated phases in frustrated Kondo lattices.

</details>


### [119] [Graph-Theoretic Detection of Hilbert Space Fragmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17951)
*A. Rutkowski,M. Mierzejewski,J. Herbrych*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出基于谱图理论的无偏方法，通过将希尔伯特空间映射为图，利用拉普拉斯谱、Fiedler向量和模块度等工具诊断量子多体系统中的Hilbert空间碎裂（包括精确碎裂和近碎裂），并将其应用于t-J模型和Hubbard链，证明了该方法的通用性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有识别Hilbert空间碎裂的方法通常依赖于守恒律的先验知识或模型特定的解析洞察，缺乏通用且无偏的诊断工具。

Method: 将多体基态视为图的顶点、哈密顿量矩阵元视为边，将Hilbert空间的连通结构映射为图；利用谱图理论中的拉普拉斯谱、Fiedler向量和模块度等工具分析图的连通分量和社区结构，从而识别精确碎裂（不连通分量）和近碎裂（弱连通社区）。

Result: 在t-J模型及其微扰中，图论诊断可靠地识别了碎裂和近碎裂结构，并捕捉了支配系统演化的动力学时间尺度层级。在Hubbard链中，该方法揭示了与双子动力学和自旋构型相关的近解耦子空间。

Conclusion: 谱图分析是一种通用且可扩展的工具，可用于诊断复杂量子多体系统中的碎裂和近似动力学约束。

Abstract: Hilbert-space fragmentation provides a mechanism for ergodicity breaking in quantum many-body systems even in the absence of disorder, leading to dynamically disconnected sectors and strong memory of initial conditions. However, identifying such structures is often challenging and typically relies on prior knowledge of conservation laws or model-specific analytical insight. Here we introduce an unbiased approach based on spectral graph theory and, within this framework, formulate the concept of nearly fragmented systems, in which perturbative processes couple otherwise fragmented sectors while preserving their dynamical imprint. By representing basis states as vertices and Hamiltonian matrix elements as edges, we map the connectivity structure of the many-body Hilbert space onto a graph and analyze it using tools such as the Laplacian spectrum, Fiedler vectors, and modularity. Exact fragmentation corresponds to disconnected graph components, while nearly fragmented systems manifest as weakly connected communities whose structure can still be resolved spectrally. Applying this framework to the one-dimensional $t$-$J$ model and its perturbations, we demonstrate that graph-theoretic diagnostics reliably identify both fragmented and nearly fragmented Hilbert-space structures and capture the hierarchy of dynamical time scales that governs the system's evolution. We further show that the method extends beyond kinetically constrained models by applying it directly to the Hubbard chain, where it reveals the emergence of nearly decoupled subspaces associated with doublon dynamics and spin configurations. Our results establish the spectral graph analysis as a general and scalable tool for diagnosing fragmentation and approximate dynamical constraints in complex quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [120] [Exact Organization of Density Matrices and Entanglement Structure in the Kitaev Spin Liquid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18002)
*Chen-Chih Wang,Yi-Ping Huang,Sungkit Yip*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文给出了自旋1/2 Kitaev自旋液体密度矩阵的精确形式，揭示了其由规范结构决定的弦算符等价类组织方式，证明了约化密度矩阵存在对称性分辨的块对角结构导致纠缠谱的简并，并将纠缠熵分解为规范部分和物质部分，同时扩展到奇数格点子系统，揭示了纠缠谱与费米子奇偶性的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自旋框架下精确理解Kitaev蜂窝模型的纠缠结构，特别是密度矩阵的显式形式及其与规范结构的关系，以及纠缠谱简并和纠缠熵可分离性的深层机制。

Method: 利用自旋算符表示Kitaev自旋液体的精确密度矩阵形式，结合规范理论的精确高斯定律和1-形式Wilson对称性，分析约化密度矩阵的对称性分辨块对角结构。

Result: 密度矩阵由规范结构相关的弦算符等价类自然组织；约化密度矩阵具有对称性分辨的块对角结构导致纠缠谱的广泛简并；纠缠熵可分离为规范部分和物质部分；奇数格点子系统纠缠谱与费米子奇偶性存在关联。

Conclusion: Kitaev自旋液体的纠缠结构由其底层规范对称性主导，约化密度矩阵的块对角结构解释了纠缠熵的规范-物质分离性，且纠缠谱与费米子奇偶性的关系为理解拓扑相的纠缠特性提供了新视角。

Abstract: We give an exact form of the density matrix of the spin-1/2 Kitaev spin liquid represented in terms of spin operators and study the entanglement structures of the Kitaev honeycomb model within the spin framework. We show that the density matrix is naturally organized by equivalence classes of string operators associated with the underlying gauge structure of the model. With the explicit form of the density matrix, plus the exact Gauss law of the emergent gauge theory and the exact 1-form Wilson symmetry in the Kitaev model, we demonstrate the existence of the underlying symmetry-resolved block-diagonal structure of the reduced density matrix, which gives rise to the extensive degeneracy in the entanglement spectrum. The block-diagonal structure is then proven to be responsible for the separability of the entanglement entropy into the gauge and matter parts. Furthermore, we extend the formalism to subsystems with an odd number of lattice sites, revealing a relation between the entanglement spectrum and the fermion parity that is seldom mentioned in the literature.

</details>


### [121] [Charge order on a triangular lattice with Mott physics and arbitrary charge density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18575)
*Aleksey Alekseev,Agnieszka Cichy,Konrad Jerzy Kapcia*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用动力学平均场理论（DMFT）研究三角晶格扩展Hubbard模型中的电荷有序现象。通过考虑最近邻相互作用，发现了包含丰富特征的相图，包括两种不同类型的弹球液相（电荷转移驱动和Mott局域化驱动）、相变阶数的变化（从连续到不连续）以及强粒子-空穴不对称性。利用简单平均场近似（MFA）可以更好地理解相图的多种特征，且MFA和原子极限模型能较好地预测DMFT相图。此外，DMFT还发现了电子掺杂侧的一个小区域中间金属相这一未预期的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三角晶格系统因各种阻挫诱导和强关联效应而备受关注，本文旨在通过研究扩展Hubbard模型中的电荷有序现象，深入理解该系统中的丰富物理行为。

Method: 采用动力学平均场理论（DMFT）研究三角晶格上的扩展Hubbard模型，同时考虑最近邻相互作用，并与简单平均场近似（MFA）和原子极限模型的结果进行对比分析。

Result: 得到了包含丰富特征的相图，包括两种弹球液相（电荷转移驱动和Mott局域化驱动）、相变阶数的变化（连续到不连续）、强粒子-空穴不对称性，以及在电子掺杂侧发现了一个小区域的中间金属相。

Conclusion: MFA和原子极限模型能较好地预测DMFT相图的主要特征，但DMFT也揭示了一些未预期的新现象（如电子掺杂侧的中间金属相），表明DMFT在处理三角晶格强关联系统时具有超越简单近似方法的优势。

Abstract: Triangular-lattice systems attract a lot of attention due to various frustration-induced and strongly correlated effects. Here, we focus on the charge-ordering phenomenon by means of investigation of the extended Hubbard model with dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT). By considering the intersite nearest-neighbor interaction we have found a very rich phase diagram that contains large number of features, phases, and phase transitions. Among them are pinball-liquid (PL) phases where we distinguish charge-transfer-driven and Mott-localization-driven PLs; phase transitions that change their order as model parameters evolve (from discontinuous to continuous); very strong particle-hole asymmetry. Various features of the phase diagram are found to be better understood by means of the simple mean-field approximation (MFA). Moreover, besides helping with interpretation of the phase diagram, the MFA results together with results for the atomic-limit model are found to be able to set rather good expectations on how the DMFT phase diagram should look like. Nevertheless, a few features were not expected and are found within the DMFT, such as a small-region intermediate metallic phase on an electron-doped side of the phase diagram.

</details>


### [122] [Pressure Effects on CeMnSi: Evolution of Ce 4f and Mn 3d Electronic States and Negative Thermal Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18230)
*Sae Nishiyama,Haruka Mima,Jun-ichi Hayashi,Keiki Takeda,Chihiro Sekine,Yoshiya Uwatoko,Hiroki Takahashi,Hiroshi Tanida,Yukihiro Kawamura*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究通过电阻率和XRD实验，研究了压力对重费米子反铁磁体CeMnSi的影响。发现Mn的反铁磁序在约1.3 GPa被抑制，出现压力诱导的新异常（T_M ~ 97 K），并在0.7 GPa附近观察到非费米液体行为。高于5.7 GPa时电阻率呈现更金属性行为。低温负热膨胀现象支持重费米子基态的形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究压力对重费米子反铁磁体CeMnSi中Mn磁态、重费米子态稳定性以及电输运性质的影响。

Method: 采用电阻率测量和粉末X射线衍射技术，在不同压力条件下研究CeMnSi的物理性质变化。

Result: 1) Mn反铁磁序（T_N ~ 240 K）在P_c ~ 1.3 GPa被迅速抑制并消失；2) 出现压力诱导的新异常T_M ~ 97 K，并随压力升高向高温移动；3) 在0.7 GPa附近观察到近乎T线性的非费米液体行为；4) 高于结构转变压力P_s ~ 5.7 GPa时，电阻率在T_M以下显著降低并呈现更金属性行为；5) 常压下CeMnSi在~40 K以下表现出负热膨胀，而LaMnSi中无此现象。

Conclusion: Mn磁态的转变可能反映了磁对称性的改变，进而影响重费米子态的稳定性。负热膨胀现象支持CeMnSi中重费米子基态的形成。

Abstract: We investigated pressure effects on the nontrivial heavy-fermion antiferromagnet CeMnSi by means of electrical resistivity and powder X-ray diffraction. With increasing pressure, the antiferromagnetic order of Mn (T_N ~ 240 K at ambient pressure) is rapidly suppressed and disappears at P_c ~ 1.3 GPa. Instead, a pressure-induced anomaly appears at T_M ~ 97 K and shifts to higher temperatures with increasing pressure. The switching of the Mn magnetic state may reflect a modification of the magnetic symmetry of the system, which could influence the stability of the heavy-fermion state. In the low-pressure region, non-Fermi-liquid-like behavior characterized by nearly T-linear resistivity is observed around 0.7 GPa. In addition, the resistivity shows a marked reduction below T_M and a qualitative change toward more metallic behavior above the structural transition pressure P_s ~ 5.7 GPa. At ambient pressure, CeMnSi exhibits negative thermal expansion below ~40 K, which is absent in LaMnSi, supporting the formation of a heavy-fermion ground state.

</details>


### [123] [Interlayer electronic coherence links magnetism and superconductivity in Ruddlesden-Popper nickelates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18524)
*Feiyang Liu,Lixing Chen,Enkang Zhang,Ying-Jie Zhang,Jun Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过高精度六端法测量双层和三层Ruddlesden-Popper镍酸盐单晶的面内与面外电阻率，揭示了强电子各向异性、层间输运的相干-非相干转变，并发现超导转变温度与各向异性呈反相关，表明层间相干性是连接磁关联和超导电性的关键组织参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ruddlesden-Popper镍酸盐中电子维度如何影响磁性和超导电性仍未明确，现有四探针测量低估了各向异性，亟需高精度方法揭示内在电子输运特性。

Method: 采用六端几何构型在同一单晶上自洽测量面内电阻率ρ∥和面外电阻率ρ⊥，最小化强各向异性导体中电流重分布带来的不确定度。

Result: 双层和三层镍酸盐均呈现强电子各向异性；ρ⊥呈非单调温度依赖，表现层间输运的相干-非相干转变；超导Tc与环境压强下电阻率各向异性呈反相关；ρ⊥对磁序和密度波序具有高灵敏度和选择性。

Conclusion: 层间相干性是追踪磁关联和连接超导电性的关键参数，为镍酸盐高温超导微观理论提供了严格约束。

Abstract: The extent to which electronic dimensionality influences magnetism and superconductivity in Ruddlesden-Popper (RP) nickelates remains unsettled. Here we report high-precision crystallographic-axis-resolved dc transport measurements on high-quality single crystals of bilayer and trilayer RP nickelates. Using a six-terminal geometry, we self-consistently determine the intrinsic in-plane ($ρ_\parallel$) and out-of-plane ($ρ_\bot$) resistivities on the same crystal, while minimizing uncertainties associated with current redistribution in highly anisotropic conductors. We uncover strong intrinsic electronic anisotropy in both bilayer and trilayer nickelates, in contrast to the weak anisotropy inferred from conventional four-probe measurements. Moreover, $ρ_\bot$ exhibits a nonmonotonic temperature dependence, revealing a universal coherent-to-incoherent crossover in interlayer transport. Across the RP nickelate series, the maximum superconducting transition temperature ($T_c$) observed under pressure is inversely correlated with the ambient-pressure resistivity anisotropy, suggesting that stronger interlayer electronic coherence is favorable for superconductivity. In addition, $ρ_\bot$ serves as an exceptionally sensitive and selective probe of magnetic and density-wave orders, exhibiting pronounced anomalies, whereas only weak signatures are observed in $ρ_\parallel$. Our results highlight interlayer coherence as a key organizing parameter that both tracks the relevant magnetic correlations and is closely tied to superconductivity, providing stringent constraints on microscopic theories of high-$T_c$ superconductivity in nickelates.

</details>
